# Theorem harvest — index

Every theorem stated anywhere in this workspace, one Markdown file per theorem, harvested mechanically from the source documents. See [SPEC.md](SPEC.md) for what counts as a theorem and how statuses are assigned.

**1210 theorems** with index rows, in **4** theorem files (4 files without a row: `AUDIT-003-cited-sources.md`, `AUDIT-verification-labels-002.md`, `AUDIT-verification-labels.md`, `ROOT-INDEX.md`), from 120 source documents.  Counts below are recomputed from the rows by `research/twin-prime-parity/002/update_index_header.py`.

| Theorem | Kind | Status | Source | Summary |

| [The term is the principal-branch power](lean/lean-rh044-smoothing-zeta-term-principal-branch-power.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean, no sorry) | [Rh044/Smoothing.lean](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh044/lean/Rh044/Smoothing.lean) | For `n ≥ 1`, `zetaTerm s n = (n : ℂ) ^ (-s)`: the exponential Dirichlet term is the principal-branch power |

| [Two-weight triangle bound](lean/lean-rh044-smoothing-two-weight-triangle-bound.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean, no sorry) | [Rh044/Smoothing.lean](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh044/lean/Rh044/Smoothing.lean) | `‖smoothedSum N φ s − smoothedSum N ψ s‖ ≤ ∑_{n≤N} ‖φ(n/N) − ψ(n/N)‖ · ‖zetaTerm s n‖` |
| [Radial decay](lean/lean-rh044-smoothing-radial-decay.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean, no sorry) | [Rh044/Smoothing.lean](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh044/lean/Rh044/Smoothing.lean) | `‖zetaTerm (σ + t·I) n‖ = (n : ℝ) ^ (-σ)` for `n ≥ 1`; the `t = 0`, `≤` and cpow forms are folded in |
| [Absolute convergence of the tail for `σ > 1`](lean/lean-rh044-smoothing-tail-absolute-convergence.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean, no sorry) | [Rh044/Smoothing.lean](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh044/lean/Rh044/Smoothing.lean) | `∑' n, ‖zetaTerm (σ : ℂ) (n + 1)‖` is summable for `σ > 1`, by comparison with the real `p`-series |
| [The error bound with the radial decay substituted](lean/lean-rh044-smoothing-error-bound-rpow.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean, no sorry) | [Rh044/Smoothing.lean](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh044/lean/Rh044/Smoothing.lean) | At `s = σ + t·I`: `‖zetaSum N s − smoothedSum N φ s‖ ≤ ∑_{n≤N} ‖1 − φ (n/N)‖ · n^{−σ}` |
| [Split into `zetaSum`-type pieces](lean/lean-rh044-smoothing-quadratic-weight-split.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean, no sorry) | [Rh044/Smoothing.lean](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh044/lean/Rh044/Smoothing.lean) | `smoothedSum N quadWeight s` equals the weight-`u` sum minus the weight-`u²` sum, `u (1 − u) = u − u²` |
| [The weight defect of the quadratic weight in closed form](lean/lean-rh044-smoothing-quadratic-weight-defect.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean, no sorry) | [Rh044/Smoothing.lean](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh044/lean/Rh044/Smoothing.lean) | `1 − (n/N)(1 − n/N) = (N² − nN + n²)/N²` for `N ≠ 0`; also with the corrected `u²`-completed numerator |

| [I8 — the finite-r means are not −1 (no convergence claim either way)](tpp/tpp-rh40-rounds-truths-i8-finite-r-means-open.md) | Claim | **SETTLED (2026-10-11 attack; OP-33).** (a) **PROVED** (Lean, `Rh42.step_bound`, `rounds/rh42/lean/Rh42/PrecisionFloor.lean`, axiom-clean): if `r_0 ≤ 1/10`, `r_{k+1} ≤ r_k²` and `10^{−D} < r_{K−1}` then `2^{K−1} ≤ D`, so `D` digits reach only `K ≤ log₂ D + 1` steps — the source's `7–13 steps before underflow` is a theorem, not a bug. (b) Means of `log c_k` over `10⁴` prescribed steps converge to the mean of `log α_k` (`1/2→−0.6931`, `1/4→−1.3863`, `1/10→−2.3026`); **none is `−1`** — the source's claim is confirmed but re-attributed (no universal limit exists; the finite-`K` mean is strictly below the `s`-mean by the un-decayed gap `Γ_K/K`). See `research/attacks/OP-28-30-33.md`. | [rh40 truths](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh40/rounds/truths.md) | The finite-r means are not −1, measured on only 7–13 steps before underflow; no convergence claim either way |

| [Correction 4 — Theorem 1 is stated with a redundant hypothesis](tpp/tpp-rh41-lean-readme-correction-4-theorem-1-redundant-hypothesis.md) | Claim | PROVED (Lean) | [rh41 lean README](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh41/lean/README.md) | `L ≥ a_m + 1` suffices; `L ≥ span` is not needed |
| [Correction 5 — Theorem 7 needs strict hypotheses](tpp/tpp-rh41-lean-readme-correction-5-theorem-7-strict-hypotheses.md) | Claim | PROVED (Lean) | [rh41 lean README](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh41/lean/README.md) | The maps need `1/(n+2) < r` and `{q²/s} ≠ 0`, the integral case being the terminating step |
| [Correction 6 — the literal phrasing of Theorem 12](tpp/tpp-rh41-lean-readme-correction-6-theorem-12-literal-phrasing.md) | Claim | PROVED (Lean) | [rh41 lean README](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh41/lean/README.md) | The truncation `N` must be allowed to grow with `L`: `p(L) < c·L` for some `1 ≤ L ≤ N` |
| [The auxiliary general bound p(L) ≤ a_m + 2](tpp/tpp-rh41-lean-readme-auxiliary-general-bound.md) | Lemma | PROVED (Lean, no sorry) | [rh41 lean README](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh41/lean/README.md) | `p(L) ≤ a_m + 2` for every `L`, making the Corollary 4 maximum exact |

| [The counting formula (T2/T3, the robust core)](tpp/tpp-rh45-truths-counting-formula.md) | Theorem | VERIFIED (numerical, twice; exact form to `1e−20`) | [rh45 truths](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh45/truths.md) | `N(T) = θ(T)/π + 1 + S(T) = (T/2π)log(T/2πe) + 7/8 + S(T) + O(1/T)` |

| [Parity problem (T4, negative)](tpp/tpp-rh45-truths-no-parity-advance.md) | Claim | PROVED (paper argument, structural; numerical measurements) | [rh45 truths](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh45/truths.md) | No advance: `Λ`/`μ`/`λ` weights cannot separate `Ω`-parity; counts `14688` vs `23835`, `Σλ(n)λ(n+2) = −940` |
| [C4.1 (`N ≳ T²` suffices) is refuted](tpp/tpp-rh45-truths-sqrt-n-cost-criterion-refuted.md) | Claim | REFUTED (numerical: no sign change within `10^{−3}` of `γ₁` for `N` up to `10^6`) | [rh45 truths](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh45/truths.md) | `Re S_N(t₁) = 0.644, 6.986, 12.539, 36.052` at `N = 10^3…10^6`, growing like `0.04√N` |

| [§1.1 — the trivial two-point bounds `\|S(x,h)\| ≤ x`, `S(x,0) = x`](tpp/tpp-rh46-readme-trivial-two-point-bounds.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean: `corr_abs_le`, `corr_zero`) | [rh46 README](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh46/README.md) | `\|S(x,h)\| ≤ x` for every `h, x`; `S(x,0) = x` because `λ(n)² = 1` |
| [§1.1 — the correlation of `λ ≡ 1` is exactly `x`, so `o(x)` is not formal](tpp/tpp-rh46-readme-correlation-zero-asymptotic-false.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean: `corrAsymptotic_zero_false`) | [rh46 README](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh46/README.md) | `o(x)` is not a formal consequence of multiplicativity plus boundedness |
| [§1.5 — `n ↦ λ(n)λ(n+2)` is not multiplicative (no Euler product)](tpp/tpp-rh46-readme-shift-product-not-multiplicative.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean: `shiftProd_not_completely_multiplicative`) | [rh46 README](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh46/README.md) | The shifted product has no Euler product; the Perron/ζ route was withdrawn |
| [§1.4 — prefix agreement does not determine the correlation asymptotic](tpp/tpp-rh46-readme-prefix-agreement-indeterminacy.md) | Theorem | PROVED (Lean: `slice_agree`, `corrSlice_eq_corr_of_lt`) + VERIFIED (auditor A1, `10⁸` prefixes) | [rh46 README](../research/twin-prime-parity/rounds/rh46/README.md) | `λ^{(a)}` agrees with `λ` on every prefix shorter than `a`, yet the correlations differ from `a` on |

## Cross-document repeats

The same statement is stated as a result in more than one source document (for example a classical theorem quoted in two notes, or a round's result restated in a later ledger). Each occurrence keeps its own file and its own source link.

- **Corollary 4** — [tpp-rh41-truths](tpp/tpp-rh41-truths-corollary-4-max-complexity.md), [tpp-rh42-truths](tpp/tpp-rh42-truths-corollary-4-max-plateau.md)
- **GOR⁺ Lemma 2.3 — the renormalised family against Hermite** — [xihorn-h-track](xihorn/xihorn-h-track-gor-lemma-2-3.md), [xihorn-todo](xihorn/xihorn-todo-gor-lemma-2-3.md)
- **GOR⁺ Theorem 1.1 — n ≥ c·e^d forces hyperbolicity** — [xihorn-h-track](xihorn/xihorn-h-track-gor-theorem-1-1.md), [xihorn-rust-results](xihorn/xihorn-rust-results-gor-theorem-1-1.md), [xihorn-todo](xihorn/xihorn-todo-gor-theorem-1-1.md)
- **M1 — HANDOFF.md §3.1's closed forms are false** — [tpp-rh40-readme](tpp/tpp-rh40-readme-m1-piecewise-closed-forms-refuted.md), [tpp-rh40-round01-brief](tpp/tpp-rh40-round01-brief-m1-piecewise-closed-forms-false.md), [tpp-rh40-round01](tpp/tpp-rh40-round01-m1-handoff-closed-forms-false.md)
- **O'S Corollary 3.8 — J hyperbolic implies P hyperbolic** — [xihorn-h-track](xihorn/xihorn-h-track-os-corollary-3-8.md), [xihorn-todo](xihorn/xihorn-todo-os-corollary-3-8.md)
- **O'S Theorem 1.3 — the exponential made polynomial** — [xihorn-h-track](xihorn/xihorn-h-track-os-theorem-1-3.md), [xihorn-rust-results](xihorn/xihorn-rust-results-os-theorem-1-3.md), [xihorn-todo](xihorn/xihorn-todo-os-theorem-1-3.md)
